# Generated modern AI competitor companies
# One centralized file. Existing exact company keys use REPLACE:; new keys do not.
# Tier I = +100% primary throughput; Tier II = +50%; Tier III = +25%.

# United States — Boeing — Tier 1
REPLACE:company_boeing = {
	background = "gfx/interface/icons/company_icons/company_backgrounds/comp_illu_harbor_shipbuilding.dds"
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/company_icons/modern_company_icons/boeing.png"
	category = bureaucrat_owned
	flavored_company = yes

	building_types = {
		building_aeroplanes_factory
		building_shipyard
	}

	extension_building_types = {
		building_engines_factory
		building_port
		building_steel_factory
	}

	prestige_goods_trigger = { }
	possible_prestige_goods = {
		prestige_good_generic_aeroplanes
	}

	potential = {
		country_has_primary_culture = cu:anglo_american
	}

	attainable = {
		hidden_trigger = {
			any_scope_state = {
				state_region = s:STATE_DISTRICT_OF_COLUMBIA
			}
		}
	}

	possible = {
		any_scope_state = {
			is_incorporated = yes
			any_scope_building = {
				OR = {
					is_building_type = building_aeroplanes_factory
					is_building_type = building_shipyard
				}
				level >= 3
			}
		}
	}

	can_establish_in = {
		is_incorporated = yes
	}

	preferred_headquarters = {
		STATE_WASHINGTON
	}

	prosperity_modifier = {
		building_aeroplanes_factory_throughput_add = 0.4
		building_shipyard_throughput_add = 0.4
		country_ship_group_supply_ships_construction_efficiency_add = 0.5
		country_prestige_mult = 0.2
	}

	ai_will_do = { always = yes }
	ai_weight = {
		value = 10
	}
}

	background = "gfx/interface/icons/company_icons/company_backgrounds/comp_illu_manufacturing_light.dds"
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/company_icons/modern_company_icons/luxottica.png"
	category = shopkeeper_owned
	flavored_company = yes

	building_types = {
		building_groceries_factory
		building_textile_mill
		building_furniture_factory
		building_liquor_factory
		building_art_academy
	}

	extension_building_types = {
		building_retail_services_sector
		building_media_services_sector
		building_trade_center
	}

	prestige_goods_trigger = { }
	possible_prestige_goods = {
		prestige_good_modern_italian_luxury_goods
	}

	potential = {
		country_has_primary_culture = cu:italian
	}

	attainable = {
		hidden_trigger = {
			any_scope_state = {
				state_region = s:STATE_LAZIO
			}
		}
	}

	possible = {
		any_scope_state = {
			is_incorporated = yes
			any_scope_building = {
				OR = {
					is_building_type = building_groceries_factory
					is_building_type = building_textile_mill
					is_building_type = building_furniture_factory
					is_building_type = building_liquor_factory
					is_building_type = building_art_academy
				}
				level >= 3
			}
		}
	}

	can_establish_in = {
		is_incorporated = yes
	}

	preferred_headquarters = {
		STATE_LAZIO
	}

	prosperity_modifier = {
		building_groceries_factory_throughput_add = 0.4
		building_textile_mill_throughput_add = 0.4
		building_furniture_factory_throughput_add = 0.4
		building_liquor_factory_throughput_add = 0.4
		building_art_academy_throughput_add = 0.4
		state_standard_of_living_add = 4
		country_prestige_mult = 0.2
	}

	ai_will_do = { always = yes }
	ai_weight = {
		value = 10
	}
}

	background = "gfx/interface/icons/company_icons/company_backgrounds/comp_illu_oil_drills.dds"
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/company_icons/modern_company_icons/repsol.png"
	category = bureaucrat_owned
	flavored_company = yes

	building_types = {
		building_iron_mine
		building_coal_mine
		building_lead_mine
		building_sulfur_mine
		building_gold_mine
		building_gold_field
		building_oil_rig
	}

	extension_building_types = {
		building_railway
		building_port
		building_power_plant
	}

	prestige_goods_trigger = { }
	possible_prestige_goods = { }

	potential = {
		country_has_primary_culture = cu:spanish
	}

	attainable = {
		hidden_trigger = {
			any_scope_state = {
				state_region = s:STATE_NEW_CASTILE
			}
		}
	}

	possible = {
		any_scope_state = {
			is_incorporated = yes
			any_scope_building = {
				OR = {
					is_building_type = building_iron_mine
					is_building_type = building_coal_mine
					is_building_type = building_lead_mine
					is_building_type = building_sulfur_mine
					is_building_type = building_gold_mine
					is_building_type = building_gold_field
					is_building_type = building_oil_rig
				}
				level >= 3
			}
		}
	}

	can_establish_in = {
		is_incorporated = yes
	}

	preferred_headquarters = {
		STATE_NEW_CASTILE
	}

	prosperity_modifier = {
		building_iron_mine_throughput_add = 0.1
		building_coal_mine_throughput_add = 0.1
		building_lead_mine_throughput_add = 0.1
		building_sulfur_mine_throughput_add = 0.1
		building_gold_mine_throughput_add = 0.1
		building_gold_field_throughput_add = 0.1
		building_oil_rig_throughput_add = 0.1
		country_minting_mult = 0.05
		state_infrastructure_mult = 0.1
	}

	ai_will_do = { always = yes }
	ai_weight = {
		value = 3
	}
}

	background = "gfx/interface/icons/company_icons/company_backgrounds/comp_illu_manufacturing_heavy.dds"
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/company_icons/modern_company_icons/mobarakeh_steel.png"
	flavored_company = yes

	building_types = {
		building_steel_factory
		building_industrial_chemicals_factory
		building_plastics_factory
		building_synthetic_rubber_factory
	}

	extension_building_types = {
		building_engines_factory
		building_construction_sector
		building_railway
	}

	prestige_goods_trigger = { }
	possible_prestige_goods = { }

	potential = {
		country_has_primary_culture = cu:persian
	}

	attainable = {
		hidden_trigger = {
			any_scope_state = {
				state_region = s:STATE_IRAKAJEMI
			}
		}
	}

	possible = {
		any_scope_state = {
			is_incorporated = yes
			any_scope_building = {
				OR = {
					is_building_type = building_steel_factory
					is_building_type = building_industrial_chemicals_factory
					is_building_type = building_plastics_factory
					is_building_type = building_synthetic_rubber_factory
				}
				level >= 3
			}
		}
	}

	can_establish_in = {
		is_incorporated = yes
	}

	preferred_headquarters = {
		STATE_ISFAHAN
	}

	prosperity_modifier = {
		building_steel_factory_throughput_add = 0.1
		building_industrial_chemicals_factory_throughput_add = 0.1
		building_plastics_factory_throughput_add = 0.1
		building_synthetic_rubber_factory_throughput_add = 0.1
		state_construction_mult = 0.05
		state_infrastructure_mult = 0.05
	}

	ai_will_do = { always = yes }
	ai_weight = {
		value = 3
	}
}

# Iran — National Iranian Oil Company — Tier 1

	background = "gfx/interface/icons/company_icons/company_backgrounds/comp_illu_oil_drills.dds"

	flavored_company = yes

		building_lead_mine

		building_gold_field
		building_oil_rig
	}

	extension_building_types = {
		building_railway
		building_port
		building_power_plant
	}

	prestige_goods_trigger = { }
	possible_prestige_goods = {
		prestige_good_generic_oil
	}

	potential = {
		country_has_primary_culture = cu:persian
	}

	attainable = {
		hidden_trigger = {
			any_scope_state = {
				state_region = s:STATE_IRAKAJEMI
			}
		}
	}

	possible = {
		any_scope_state = {
			is_incorporated = yes
			any_scope_building = {
				OR = {
					is_building_type = building_iron_mine
					is_building_type = building_coal_mine
					is_building_type = building_lead_mine
					is_building_type = building_sulfur_mine
					is_building_type = building_gold_mine
					is_building_type = building_gold_field
					is_building_type = building_oil_rig
				}
				level >= 3
			}
		}
	}

	can_establish_in = {
		is_incorporated = yes
	}

	preferred_headquarters = {
		STATE_IRAKAJEMI
	}

	prosperity_modifier = {
		building_iron_mine_throughput_add = 0.4
		building_coal_mine_throughput_add = 0.4
		building_lead_mine_throughput_add = 0.4
		building_sulfur_mine_throughput_add = 0.4
		building_gold_mine_throughput_add = 0.4
		building_gold_field_throughput_add = 0.4
		building_oil_rig_throughput_add = 0.4
		country_minting_mult = 0.2
